- **verbose**: The generator can only work with what it is handed. - **verbose**: Debugging is the scientific method under time pressure. ([snoodleboot-io/prompticorn](https://github.com/snoodleboot-io/prompticorn/tree/HEAD/prompticorn/skills/debugging-methodology/verbose) · ★2)
- **verbose**: Relational modelling lets you defer query design: normalize the entities, and the optimizer will find a plan for whatever you ask later. Non-relational stores remove that safety net. There is no join and often no ad-hoc filter, so the physical layout *is* the query plan. Design it from the queries or you will discover, in production, that a required read is impossible without a full scan. ([snoodleboot-io/prompticorn](https://github.com/snoodleboot-io/prompticorn/tree/HEAD/prompticorn/skills/nosql-database-selection/verbose) · ★2)
- **verbose**: The most expensive database mistakes are made before a single row is written, by choosing the engine from familiarity or hype and then discovering the data does not fit. Invert it. - **verbose**: A container image is an attack surface you chose. - **verbose**: The model is a dependency you do not control and cannot version-pin by default, so your telemetry is the only way you learn it changed. ([snoodleboot-io/prompticorn](https://github.com/snoodleboot-io/prompticorn/tree/HEAD/prompticorn/skills/llm-observability/verbose) · ★2)
- **verbose**: The single highest-leverage decision is recognizing that most of what teams call "state" is not client state at all. ([snoodleboot-io/prompticorn](https://github.com/snoodleboot-io/prompticorn/tree/HEAD/prompticorn/skills/state-management-architecture/verbose) · ★2)
- **verbose**: The purpose of a postmortem is to change the system, and that only works if people tell you what actually happened. Blame destroys that input directly: an engineer who expects to be named for a mistake reports incidents later, describes them more cautiously, and omits the detail that would have explained the failure. The cost is not abstract — it shows up as longer detection times on the next inci ([snoodleboot-io/prompticorn](https://github.com/snoodleboot-io/prompticorn/tree/HEAD/prompticorn/skills/continuous-improvement/verbose) · ★2)
- **verbose**: These three are not interchangeable, and picking the wrong one is the root of a surprising share of order-dependent failures. ([snoodleboot-io/prompticorn](https://github.com/snoodleboot-io/prompticorn/tree/HEAD/prompticorn/skills/test-data-strategies/verbose) · ★2)

- **verbose**: The purpose of a readiness review is to produce a decision that could have gone the other way. - **verbose**: A validation *boundary* is any point where data crosses from a system you do not control into one you do: an HTTP response, an S3 drop, a Kafka topic, a partner SFTP file, a form submission. - **verbose**: A stakeholder is anyone whose plans change based on what your team does.
